27_leopold_hainaut_heir = {
	#布拉班特公爵，刚果屠夫早逝的亲儿子
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BEL
				c:BEL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#比利时成立尼德兰联省
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BEL
				}
				exists = c:UNL
				c:UNL = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BEL
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1869.1.22
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_leopold_hainaut_heir
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_leopold_hainaut_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的父亲成为继承人
				heir ?= {
					has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1859.6.12
		game_date < 1869.1.22
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			#除非当前统治者是第一代比利时国王或者利奥波德二世，利奥波德王子才成为继承人
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			#如果前两任比利时国王早死，利奥波德王子直接成为统治者
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
				ruler = yes
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_leopold_hainaut_heir
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_philippe_bel_heir = {
	#菲利普亲王，利奥波德二世的兄弟
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BEL
				c:BEL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#比利时成立尼德兰联省
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BEL
				}
				exists = c:UNL
				c:UNL = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BEL
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1875.4.8
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_philippe_bel_heir
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_philippe_bel_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的兄弟和侄子
				heir ?= {
					has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1869.1.22
		game_date < 1875.4.8
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			#除非当前统治者是第一代比利时国王、利奥波德二世、利奥波德王子之一才成为继承人
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			#如果前两任比利时国王和利奥波德王子全部早死，菲利普亲王成为统治者
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_philippe_bel_heir
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_bel_baudoin_heir = {
	#阿尔贝一世哥哥，前者长子
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BEL
				c:BEL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#比利时成立尼德兰联省
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BEL
				}
				exists = c:UNL
				c:UNL = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BEL
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1891.1.23
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_bel_baudoin_heir
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_baudoin_bel_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1891.1.23
		game_date >= 1869.6.3
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_baudouin_I_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_bel_baudouin_I_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_bel_baudoin_heir
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

	#阿尔贝一世
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BEL
				c:BEL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {

				NOT = {
					exists = c:BEL
				}

				c:UNL = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BEL
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_albert_1_heir
		}
		game_date < 1934.2.17
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_albert_1_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
				}

					has_template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bel_baudouin_I_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1875.4.8
		game_date < 1934.2.17

		}
		if = {

				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {

						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BEL_leopold_II_saxe_coburg_gotha_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_leopold_hainaut_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_philippe_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bel_baudouin_I_heir_template

			}

27_leopold_3_heir = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:BEL
				c:BEL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#比利时成立尼德兰联省
				NOT = {
					exists = c:BEL
				}
				exists = c:UNL
				c:UNL = ROOT
				was_formed_from = BEL
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy

			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy

27_brazil_heir_1 = {
	#佩德罗二世早夭的儿子，有原版事件会杀继承人
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:BRZ
		c:BRZ = ROOT
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1847.6.11
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_brazil_heir_1
		}
	}
	possible = {
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1845.2.23
		game_date < 1847.6.11
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			#除非当前统治者是佩德罗二世才成为继承人
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			#如果佩德罗二世提前死亡，小儿子直接成为统治者
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_brazil_heir_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_brazil_heir_3 = {
	#佩德罗二世早夭的第二个儿子
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:BRZ
		c:BRZ = ROOT
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1850.1.9
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_brazil_heir_3
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_brazil_heir_3_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的哥哥
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1848.7.19
		game_date < 1850.1.9
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_pedro_afonso_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_pedro_afonso_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_brazil_heir_3
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_brazil_heir_4 = {
	#伊莎贝尔儿子
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:BRZ
		c:BRZ = ROOT
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_brazil_heir_4
		}
		game_date < 1940.1.29
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_brazil_heir_4_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的妈妈和舅舅
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_brz_pedro_afonso_heir_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = brz_isabel_template
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1875.10.15
		game_date < 1940.1.29
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_pedro_afonso_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = brz_isabel_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_pedro_de_orleans_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_pedro_afonso_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_brz_alfonso_pedro_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = brz_isabel_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BRZ_emperor_pedro_02
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_brz_pedro_de_orleans_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes

27_denmark_heir_1 = {
	#丹麦国王或者斯堪的纳维亚的皇帝
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:DEN
				c:DEN = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#丹麦成立大斯
				NOT = {
					exists = c:DEN
				}
				exists = c:SCA
				c:SCA = ROOT
				was_formed_from = DEN
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1863.11.15
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_denmark_heir_1
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_denmark_heir_1_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的父亲
				heir ?= {
					has_template = DEN_christian_von_oldenburg
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1863.11.15
		game_date >= 1839.12.3
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DEN_frederik_von_oldenburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DEN_christian_von_oldenburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_den_frederik_oldenburg_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DEN_frederik_von_oldenburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DEN_christian_von_oldenburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_den_frederik_oldenburg_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_denmark_heir_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1900.7.30
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_gbr_alfred_1
		}
	}
	possible = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1864.8.6
		game_date < 1900.7.30
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = 27_gbr_alfred_i_heir_template
                                add_character_role = character_role_politician
			}
		}
	set_variable = {
			name = 27_gbr_alfred_1
			value = yes
	}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_gbr_albert_victor = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:GBR
				c:GBR = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GBR
				}
				exists = c:ENG
				c:ENG = ROOT
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1892.1.14
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_gbr_albert_victor
		}
		NOT = {
			#has_variable = 27_gbr_leopold_albany
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_gbr_albert_victor_heir_king
			has_variable = is_edward_7
			NOT = {
				#has_variable = 27_gbr_leopold_albany
			}
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = gbr_albert_edward_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1864.1.8
		game_date < 1892.1.14
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = gbr_queen_victoria_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = gbr_albert_edward_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_gbr_albert_victor_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = gbr_queen_victoria_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = gbr_albert_edward_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_gbr_albert_victor_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_gbr_albert_victor
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_gre_georges_1 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:GRE
				c:GRE = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊成立拜占庭
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:BYZ
				c:BYZ = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊成立东罗马
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:ERE
				c:ERE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊帝国
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:HEMP
				c:HEMP = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#罗马帝国，但是丹麦王子
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:SPQR
				c:SPQR = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
				is_subject = no
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
				is_subject = yes
				top_overlord = {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
		}
		game_date < 1913.3.18
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_gre_georges_1
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = grefm_is_glucksburg
		}
		#巴尔干风味的乔治一世
		is_player = no		#禁止原因是因为巴尔干两个风味都有他了，玩家有很多选择
	}
	possible = {
		is_player = no
		game_date >= 1863.1.1
		game_date < 1913.3.18
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
				is_subject = no
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
				}
				is_subject = yes
				top_overlord = {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			#旧王宫
			limit = {
				is_player = no
			}
			set_variable = grefm_palace_authorized
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy		#回归君主制
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_trait = trait_grefm_bavarocrat
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = trait_grefm_bavarocrat
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_character = {
					template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
                hidden_effect = {
		create_military_formation = {
			type = fleet
			hq_region = sr:region_balkans
			name = royal_hellenic_navy
			ship = {
				type = ship_type:ship_type_coastal_defense_ship
				count = 1
			}
		}
                }
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_gre_georges_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_gre_constantin_1 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:GRE
				c:GRE = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊成立拜占庭
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:BYZ
				c:BYZ = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊成立东罗马
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:ERE
				c:ERE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#希腊帝国
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:HEMP
				c:HEMP = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
			AND = {
				#罗马帝国，但是丹麦王子
				NOT = {
					exists = c:GRE
				}
				exists = c:SPQR
				c:SPQR = ROOT
				was_formed_from = GRE
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1923.1.11
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_gre_constantin_1
		}
	}
	possible = {
		game_date >= 1868.8.2
		game_date < 1923.1.11
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_gre_constantin_1_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = trait_grefm_bavarocrat
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_gre_konstantinos_I_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = trait_grefm_bavarocrat
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = GRE_george_i_of_greece
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_gre_konstantinos_I_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_gre_constantin_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SIC_ferdinando_di_borbone_delle_due_sicilie
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SIC_francesco_di_borbone_delle_due_sicilie
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPA_alfonso_caserta
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_sic_ferdinando_III_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SIC_ferdinando_di_borbone_delle_due_sicilie
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SIC_francesco_di_borbone_delle_due_sicilie
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPA_alfonso_caserta
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_sic_ferdinando_III_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = 27_sic_ferdinando_3
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = tur_reshad_osmanoglu_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {

				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = tur_mahmut_osmanoglu_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}

					}

					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = tur_abdulmecid_osmanoglu_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = tur_abdulaziz_osmanoglu_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {

					}
					set_character_immortal = no

				}

27_alexander_ioan_cuza = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:MOL

			}
			AND = {
				#摩尔达维亚-罗马尼亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:MOL
				}
				exists = c:ROM
				c:ROM = ROOT
				was_formed_from = MOL
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1873.5.15
		NOT = {
			has_variable = alexander_ioan_cuza
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_alexander_ioan_cuza_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_trait = persistent
					is_historical = yes
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1849.1.1
		game_date < 1873.5.15
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = c:WAL
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = meticulous
						is_historical = yes
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = persistent
						is_historical = yes
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				first_name = "ecchi_Alexander_Ioan"
				last_name = "Cuza"
				female = no
				historical = yes
				heir = yes
				ideology = ideology_kogalnitchanism
				interest_group = ig_armed_forces
				birth_date = 1820.4.1
				dna = dna_ecchi_mol_aic
				traits = {
					celebrity_commander
					charismatic
					basic_diplomat
					basic_political_operator
					engineer
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = meticulous
						is_historical = yes
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = persistent
						is_historical = yes
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				first_name = "ecchi_Alexander_Ioan"
				last_name = "Cuza"
				female = no
				historical = yes
				ruler = yes
				ideology = ideology_kogalnitchanism
				interest_group = ig_armed_forces
				birth_date = 1820.4.1
				dna = dna_ecchi_mol_aic
				traits = {
					celebrity_commander
					charismatic
					basic_diplomat
					basic_political_operator
					engineer
				}
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
		}

			value = yes
		}
	}

	}

					has_template = 27_net_carel_alexander_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1909.4.30
		game_date < 2004.3.20
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_willem_iii_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_willem_iiii_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_carel_alexander_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_wilhelmina_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_net_juliana_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_willem_iii_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_willem_iiii_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_carel_alexander_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_net_wilhelmina_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_net_juliana_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_net_juliana
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_por_pedro_5 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:POR
				c:POR = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#葡萄牙成立伊比利亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:POR
				}
				exists = c:IBE
				c:IBE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = POR
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1861.11.11
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_por_pedro_5
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1837.9.16
		game_date < 1861.11.11
	}
	when_taken = {
		#玛丽亚妈妈在的话成为继承人，否则直接当国王
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					has_template = POR_maria_ii_de_braganca
					is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = POR_maria_ii_de_braganca
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = POR_pedro_v_de_braganca_template
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_por_pedro_5
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_rom_carol_I_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_rom_carol_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_rom_ferdinand_rom = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:WAL
				c:WAL = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#瓦拉几亚-罗马尼亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:WAL
				}
				exists = c:ROM
				c:ROM = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#罗马尼亚-多瑙联邦
				NOT = {
					exists = c:WAL
				}
				NOT = {
					exists = c:ROM
				}
				exists = c:KUK
				c:KUK = ROOT
				was_formed_from = ROM
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1927.7.20
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_ferdinand_rom
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_rom_ferdinand_rom_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_rom_carol_I_heir_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1886.1.1
		game_date < 1927.7.20
		NOT = {
			is_subject_of = c:MOL
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = cautious
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
						is_historical = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_rom_carol_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_rom_ferdinand_I_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_trait = cautious
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
						is_historical = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_rom_carol_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_rom_ferdinand_I_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_ferdinand_rom
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

					exists = c:ROM

		game_date < 1953.4.4

			is_subject_of = c:MOL
		}

	}

						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_rom_ferdinand_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_rom_carol_II_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

						has_template = 27_rom_carol_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_rom_ferdinand_I_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_rom_carol_II_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {

			value = yes
		}

	#撒丁正统王室都在上面了，这些分支去当将军了
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SAR
				c:SAR = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#撒丁成立意大利
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SAR
				}
				exists = c:ITA
				c:ITA = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SAR
			}
			AND = {
				#罗马帝国，但是萨伏伊
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SAR
				}
				exists = c:SPQR
				c:SPQR = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SAR
			}
		}
		game_date < 1931.7.4
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = 27_sar_general_emafili
		}
	}
	possible = {
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				ig_approval >= 8
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1890.1.1
		game_date < 1931.7.4
	}
	when_taken = {
		create_character = {
                                                  template = ecchi_sar_Emanuele_Filiberto_template
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = 27_sar_general_emafili
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_spa_alphonse_12 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SPA
				c:SPA ?= this
			}
			AND = {
				#西班牙成立伊比利亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SPA
				}
				exists = c:IBE
				c:IBE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SPA
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1885.11.25
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_alphonse_12
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_juan_3
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_carlos_5
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_carlos_7
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_carlist_jaime_3
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = carlos_now_spa_king
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_juan_3
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_carlos_5
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_carlos_7
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_carlist_jaime_3
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1857.11.28
		game_date < 1885.11.25
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_isabel_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_amadeo_i_de_savoy_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_isabel_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_amadeo_i_de_savoy_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_spa_alphonse_12
			value = yes
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = esp.39
			popup = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_isabel_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_amadeo_i_de_savoy_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xiii_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_spa_alfonso_xiv_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_spa_jaime_iv_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_spa_juan_iii_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_isabel_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_amadeo_i_de_savoy_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xii_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = spa_alfonso_xiii_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_spa_alfonso_xiv_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_spa_jaime_iv_heir_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_spa_juan_iii_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_spa_juan_3
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_spa_juan_3 = {
	#啊，王位分支落在了卡洛斯一派了
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SPA
				c:SPA ?= this
			}
			AND = {
				#西班牙成立伊比利亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SPA
				}
				exists = c:IBE
				c:IBE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SPA
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1887.11.18
		has_variable = 27_spa_carlos_5
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_juan_3
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_jaime_4
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_alphonse_12
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_alphonse_14
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_spa_alphonse_13
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
			}
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = alphonse_now_spa_king
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_jaime_4
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_alphonse_12
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_alphonse_14
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = 27_spa_alphonse_13
			}
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = SPC_carlos_vi_de_borbon_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1861.1.13
		game_date < 1887.11.18
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_v_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_vi_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_v_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_vi_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_spa_juan_3
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

			NOT = {

			}
			NOT = {

			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}

			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {

					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_vi_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {

				heir = yes

		if = {

							has_template = SPC_carlos_vi_de_borbon_template

					}
				}
			}
			if = {

							has_template = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
						}
					}

						limit = {
							has_template = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT

			}
			else = {

				ruler ?= {
					if = {

							is_immortal = yes
						}

					template = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete

				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes

		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_spa_carlos_7
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

#卡尔十五世原版奥斯卡登基事件给，如果前期贝尔纳多特父子都死了，卡尔十五无法诞生不关我事
#贝尔纳多特父子双亡的情况下（多半内战造成的），下方奥斯卡二世会直接登基
27_swe_oskar_2 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SWE
				c:SWE = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#瑞典成立大斯
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SWE
				}
				exists = c:SCA
				c:SCA = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SWE
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1907.12.8
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_swe_oskar_2
		}
		NOT = {
			#这个变量说明原版奥斯卡二世出现了
			has_variable = charles_xv_ruler_var
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_swe_oskar_2_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1859.7.8
		game_date < 1907.12.8
	}
	when_taken = {
		set_variable = charles_xv_ruler_var
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_karl_johan_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						add_character_role = heir
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					add_character_role = heir
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					ruler = no
                                        heir = yes
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_karl_johan_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
					ruler = yes
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}

			value = yes
		}
	}

27_swe_gustaf_5 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SWE
				c:SWE = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#瑞典成立大斯
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SWE
				}
				exists = c:SCA
				c:SCA = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SWE
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1950.10.29
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_swe_gustaf_5
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = oscar_ii_ruler_var
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_swe_gustaf_5_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1872.9.18
		game_date < 1950.10.29
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
	}
	when_taken = {
		set_variable = oscar_ii_ruler_var
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_karl_johan_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						add_character_role = heir
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					add_character_role = heir
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
					ruler = no
                                        heir = yes
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_karl_johan_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_charles_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = swe_oscar_ii_bernadotte_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = swe_gustaf_v_bernadotte_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {

					ruler = yes
					on_created = {

					}

	}

			}
			AND = {
				#塞尔维亚帝国
				NOT = {
					exists = c:SER
				}
				exists = c:SRPS
				c:SRPS = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SER
				NOT = {
					was_formed_from = MON
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1940.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_ser_petar_heir
		}
	}
	possible = {
		game_date >= 1903.6.25
		game_date < 1936.1.1
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		OR = {
			is_player = no			#强行历史
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = 27_ser_petar_heir_king
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SER_milos_obrenovic
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SER_mihailo_obrenovic
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SER_milan_obrenovic
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SER_milan_iv_obrenovic
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SER_alexander_obrenovic
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		create_character = {
			template = SER_petar_i_karadordevic
			heir = no
			ruler = yes
		}
		create_character = {
			template = SER_alexander_i_karadordevic
			heir = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_ser_petar_heir
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_afg_mohammad_yahya = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:KAB
				c:KAB = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#阿富汗
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KAB
				}
				exists = c:AFG
				c:AFG = ROOT
				was_formed_from = KAB
			}
			AND = {
				#阿富汗
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KAB
				}
				exists = c:AFG
				c:AFG = ROOT
				NOT = {
					was_formed_from = KAN
				}
				NOT = {
					was_formed_from = HER
				}
				NOT = {
					was_formed_from = MAI
				}
				NOT = {
					was_formed_from = KUN
				}
			}
			AND = {
				#突厥斯坦
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KAB
				}
				exists = c:TRH
				c:TRH = ROOT
				was_formed_from = KAB
			}
			AND = {
				#中亚
				NOT = {
					exists = c:KAB
				}
				exists = c:KHO
				c:KHO = ROOT
				was_formed_from = KAB
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1911.1.1
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_afg_mohammad_yahya
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_afg_mohammad_yahya_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
						has_template = KAB_akbar_barakzai
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1866.1.1
		game_date < 1911.1.1
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dost_mohammad_khan
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = KAB_akbar_barakzai
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_afg_mohammad_yusuf_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = dost_mohammad_khan
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = KAB_akbar_barakzai
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_afg_mohammad_yusuf_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_afg_mohammad_yahya
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_nej_abdul_rahman_01_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_nej_abdulaziz_02_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_nej_saud_04_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_nej_saud_04
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_king_egy_ismail = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:EGY
				c:EGY = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#阿拉伯
				NOT = {
					exists = c:EGY
				}
				exists = c:ARA
				c:ARA = ROOT
				was_formed_from = EGY
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1895.3.2
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_king_egy_ismail
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_king_egy_ismail_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = egy_ibrahim_alawi_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date >= 1861.1.1
		game_date < 1895.3.2
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = egy_muhammad_ali_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = egy_ibrahim_alawi_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_king_egy_ismail_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = egy_muhammad_ali_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = egy_ibrahim_alawi_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_king_egy_ismail_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}

			value = yes
		}

					}

		}
		game_date < 1918.11.1
	}
	possible = {

			text = 27_viktoria_luise_heir_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_ger_friedrich_3_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_ger_wilhelm_2_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1892.9.13
		game_date < 1918.11.1
	}
	when_taken = {
                if = {
                limit = {
                        is_player = yes
                }
		create_character = {
			template = 27_ger_viktoria_luise_template
			heir = yes
		}
                }
                if = {
                limit = {
                        is_player = no
                }
		create_character = {
			template = 27_ger_viktoria_luise_template
		        heir = no
		}
                }
		set_variable = {
			name = is_viktoria_luise
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

					has_template = 27_bav_otto_i_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_bav_ludwig_iii_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1900.1.1
		game_date < 1955.8.2
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BAV_ludwig_i_von_bayern
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BAV_maximillian_von_bayern
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bav_ludwig_ii_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bav_otto_i_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_bav_ludwig_iii_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = bav_rupprecht_bayern
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_bav_rupprecht
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

saxony_heir_1 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:SAX
				c:SAX = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#萨克森-北德
				exists = c:NGF
				c:NGF = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SAX
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:GER
				c:GER = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SAX
			}
			AND = {
				#萨克森-神罗？
				exists = c:HRE
				c:HRE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = SAX
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1873.10.29
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_johann
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_sax_heir_1_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = SAX_Friedrich_August_Wettin
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1836.6.6
		game_date < 1873.10.29
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SAX_Anton_Wettin
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SAX_Friedrich_August_Wettin
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_SAX_johann_wettin_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SAX_Anton_Wettin
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = SAX_Friedrich_August_Wettin
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_SAX_johann_wettin_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_johann
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

			}

				#符腾堡-神罗？

				c:HRE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = WUR
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1891.10.6

			has_variable = is_wilhelm_2_wurt
		}

						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes

			create_character = {
				first_name = Wilhelm_II
				last_name = von_Wurttemberg
				heir = yes
				historical = yes
				culture = cu:south_german
				religion = rel:catholic
				birth_date = 1848.2.25

				interest_group = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				ideology = ideology_reformer
				traits = {
					romantic
					charismatic
					inexperienced
					hunter
					well_travelled
					surveyor
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = WUR_wilhelm_i_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = WUR_Karl_i_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				first_name = Wilhelm_II
				last_name = von_Wurttemberg
				ruler = yes
				culture = cu:south_german
				religion = rel:catholic

						has_template = BAD_Leopold_i_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BAD_Louis_ii_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_BAD_friedrich_i_baden_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_BAD_friedrich_ii_baden_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BAD_Leopold_i_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = BAD_Louis_ii_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_BAD_friedrich_i_baden_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_BAD_friedrich_ii_baden_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_friedrich_2
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

hessen_heir_1 = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:HES
				c:HES = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				#黑森-北德
				exists = c:NGF
				c:NGF = ROOT
				was_formed_from = HES
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:GER
				c:GER = ROOT
				was_formed_from = HES
			}
			AND = {
				#黑森-神罗？
				exists = c:HRE
				c:HRE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = HES
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1892.3.13
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_ludwig_hessen
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_hes_heir_1_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = HES_Ludwig_III_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1848.6.16
		game_date < 1892.3.13
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = HES_Ludwig_II_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = HES_Ludwig_III_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_HES_friedrich_ii_Hessen_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = HES_Ludwig_II_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = HES_Ludwig_III_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_HES_friedrich_ii_Hessen_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_ludwig_hessen
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_karl_ludwig_heir = {
	#鲁道夫叔叔，老皇帝弟弟
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:AUS
				c:AUS = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:SGF
				c:SGF ?= this
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:KUK
				c:KUK = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:HRE
				c:HRE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:GER
				c:GER = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1896.5.19
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = is_karl_ludwig_habsburg
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_karl_ludwig_heir_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的侄子成为继承人
				heir ?= {
					has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1889.1.30
		game_date < 1896.5.19
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#疯子
						has_template = aus_ferdinand_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#鲁道夫
						has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#约瑟夫
						has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#疯子
						has_template = aus_ferdinand_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#鲁道夫
						has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#约瑟夫
						has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = is_karl_ludwig_habsburg
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_franz_ferdinand_heir = {
	#斐迪南大公
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:AUS
				c:AUS = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:SGF
				c:SGF ?= this
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:KUK
				c:KUK = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:HRE
				c:HRE = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:GER
				c:GER = ROOT
				was_formed_from = AUS
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1914.6.28
		NOT = {
			has_global_variable = is_franz_ferdinand_habsburg
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_franz_ferdinand_heir_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1896.5.19
		game_date < 1914.6.28
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#疯子
						has_template = aus_ferdinand_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#鲁道夫
						has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#约瑟夫
						has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = aus_franz_ferdinand
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#疯子
						has_template = aus_ferdinand_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#鲁道夫
						has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#约瑟夫
						has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = aus_franz_ferdinand
					ruler = yes
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = is_franz_ferdinand_habsburg
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_aus_otto_heir_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#鲁道夫
						has_template = aus_rudolf_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#约瑟夫
						has_template = aus_franz_josef_I_habsburg_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_aus_karl_ludwig_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_franz_ferdinand
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = aus_karl_I_habsburg
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_aus_otto_heir_template
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_global_variable = {
			name = is_otto_habsburg
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}
