chi_empire2 = {
	is_shown = {
		is_ai = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:CHI
				root = c:CHI
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:ROC
				c:ROC = ROOT
			}
		}
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_chi_empire2
		}
		game_date < 1908.11.14
	}
	possible = {
		is_ai = yes
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_chi_empire2_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
				}
			}
		}
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date > 1874.8.14
		game_date < 1908.11.14
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_landowners = {
					leader = {
						is_historical = no
					}
				}
			}
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				leader = {
					save_scope_as = kill_this_normal_man_scope
				}
			}
			scope:kill_this_normal_man_scope = {
				kill_character = {
					hidden = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_character = {
					template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
					ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			#司马P社摄政王可以直接杀掉光绪
			name = is_chi_empire2
			days = 1825
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

			has_variable = heir_taisho_spawned
		}
		has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		is_player = no		#邓三科事件和原版效果都能出
	}
	possible = {
		is_player = no

				}
			}
		}
	}
	when_taken = {
		set_variable = heir_taisho_spawned
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {

					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_taisho_yamato
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_komei_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {

					any_country = {

						}
					}
				}

riben_empire66 = {
	#孝明去世直接换明治
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:JAP
		root = c:JAP
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_meiji_66
		}
		has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		game_date < 1879.8.30
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = is_meiji_restoration_complete
			has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date > 1867.1.1
		game_date < 1879.8.30
	}
	when_taken = {
		set_variable = heir_meiji_spawned
		s:STATE_KANTO = {
					random_scope_state = {
						set_hub_name = {
							type = city
							name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KANTO_city_meiji
						}
					}
		}
                if = {
                limit = {
                is_player = no
                }
		cu:japanese ?= {
			set_name_format = last_first
		}
		}
		add_modifier = modifier_regency
		every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
                                        has_template = JAP_komei_yamato
				}
				kill_character = yes
		}
		ruler ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_immortal = yes
				}
				set_character_immortal = no
			}
		}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_meiji_yamato
					ruler = yes
			                heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		remove_regency_modifier = yes
                trigger_event = { id = clever_dsk.263 }
		set_variable = {
			name = is_meiji_66
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = riben_empire51_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1853.7.23
		game_date < 1866.8.29
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_ienari_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						add_character_role = heir
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					add_character_role = heir
				}
			}
			else = {
			         create_character = {
				 template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
				 heir = yes
			}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_ienari_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = riben_empire51
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

riben_empire52 = {
	#德川庆喜
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:JAP
		c:JAP = ROOT
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date < 1913.11.22
		NOT = {
			has_variable = riben_empire52
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = riben_empire52_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date >= 1858.8.14
		game_date < 1913.11.22

		if = {

					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {

riben_empire2 = {
	#昭和成为继承人
	is_shown = {
		exists = c:JAP
		root = c:JAP
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_Showa_1
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = heir_showa_spawned
		}
		is_player = no
		has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		game_date < 1989.1.7
	}
	possible = {
		is_player = no
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = is_meiji_restoration_complete
			has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_riben_empire2_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date > 1901.4.29
		game_date < 1989.1.7
	}
	when_taken = {
		set_variable = heir_showa_spawned
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#我做的明治天皇
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#大正
						has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_showa_yamato
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
						}
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
						}
					}
					random_scope_character = {
						limit = {
							has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
						}
						transfer_character = ROOT
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					any_character_in_exile_pool = {
						has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
					}
				}
				random_character_in_exile_pool = {
					limit = {
						has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_modifier = modifier_regency
				ruler ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_immortal = yes
						}
						set_character_immortal = no
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_showa_yamato
					ruler = yes
					heir = no
					on_created = {
						set_variable = regency_complete
					}
				}
				remove_regency_modifier = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_Showa_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

	}

			text = is_meiji_restoration_complete
			has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_riben_empire3_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
				}
			}
		}
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		game_date > 1933.12.23
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						#我做的明治天皇
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#大正
						has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						#昭和
						has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_ecchi_akihito
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_showa_yamato
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_ecchi_akihito
				heir = no
				ruler = yes
				on_created = {
					set_variable = regency_complete
				}
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_akihito_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}

27_vtm_Nguyen_Phuoc_thi_heir = {
	is_shown = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:DAI
				c:DAI = ROOT
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = {
					exists = c:DAI
				}
				was_formed_from = DAI
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date < 1883.7.19
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 27_vtm_Nguyen_Phuoc_thi_heir
		}
	}
	possible = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_vtm_Nguyen_Phuoc_thi_heir_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = DAI_thieu_tri_nguyen_phuoc
				}
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_theocracy
		}
		game_date >= 1841.1.15
		game_date < 1883.7.19
	}
	when_taken = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DAI_minh_mang_nguyen_phuoc
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DAI_thieu_tri_nguyen_phuoc
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_DAI_thieu_tri_nguyen_thi
				dna = dsk_dna_ruan_fu_shi
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DAI_minh_mang_nguyen_phuoc
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = DAI_thieu_tri_nguyen_phuoc
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_regency
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_DAI_thieu_tri_nguyen_thi
				dna = dsk_dna_ruan_fu_shi
				ruler = yes
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = 27_vtm_Nguyen_Phuoc_thi_heir
			value = yes
		}
	}
	ai_chance = {
		value = 100
	}
}
